# BATINGA-ANIMAL-MEDICAL-CENTER-PET-APPOINTMENT-SYSTEM
The Batinga Animal Medical Center Pet Appointment System is a digital clinic platform that lets pet owners register, book appointments, and view medical records online. It helps veterinary staff manage client profiles, track pet health histories, and organize daily billing and schedules efficiently.

Signup

    private void jCheckBox1ActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                           
        if (jCheckBox1.isSelected()) {
            password.setEchoChar((char) 0);
            password1.setEchoChar((char) 0);
        } else {
            password.setEchoChar('•');
            password1.setEchoChar('*');
        }
    }                                          

    private void jButton3ActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                         
        loginform log = new loginform();
        log .setVisible(true);
        this.dispose();
    }                                        

    private void jButton2ActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                         
        String uN = username.getText().trim();
        String pW = new String(password.getPassword()).trim();

        if (uN.isEmpty()) {
            JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                this,
                "Username is required",
                "Error",
                JOptionPane.ERROR_MESSAGE
            );
            return;
        }

        if (pW.isEmpty()) {
            JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                this,
                "Password is required",
                "Error",
                JOptionPane.ERROR_MESSAGE
            );
            return;
        }

        try (Connection con = DBConnection.getConnection()) {

            String sql = "INSERT INTO tbl_users (username, password) VALUES (?, ?)";

            PreparedStatement pst = con.prepareStatement(sql);

            pst.setString(1, uN);
            pst.setString(2, pW);

            pst.executeUpdate();

            JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                this,
                "Account Created Successfully!",
                "Success",
                JOptionPane.INFORMATION_MESSAGE
            );

            loginform login = new loginform();
            login.setVisible(true);
            this.dispose();

        } catch (Exception e) {

            JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                this,
                "Database Error: " + e.getMessage(),
                "Error",
                JOptionPane.ERROR_MESSAGE
            );

            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }

    private void showpasswordActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                             
     if (showpassword.isSelected()) {
        password.setEchoChar((char) 0);
    } else {
        password.setEchoChar('*');
    }

    private void loginActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                      
  
    String uN = username.getText().trim();
    String pW = new String(password.getPassword()).trim();

    if (uN.isEmpty() || pW.isEmpty()) {

        JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                this,
                "Please enter your username and password.",
                "Login",
                JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE
        );

        return;
    }

    try {

        Connection con = DBConnection.getConnection();

        String sql = "SELECT * FROM tbl_users WHERE username=? AND password=?";

        PreparedStatement pst = con.prepareStatement(sql);

        pst.setString(1, uN);
        pst.setString(2, pW);

        ResultSet rs = pst.executeQuery();

        if (rs.next()) {

            int userID = rs.getInt("user_id");

            UserSession.setUser(userID, uN);

            JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                    this,
                    "Login Successful!"
            );

           new Homepage(userID).setVisible(true);
            this.dispose();

        } else {

            JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                    this,
                    "Invalid username or password",
                    "Login",
                    JOptionPane.ERROR_MESSAGE
            );
        }

        con.close();

    } catch (Exception e) {

        JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                this,
                "Error: " + e.getMessage(),
                "Login Error",
                JOptionPane.ERROR_MESSAGE
        );

        e.printStackTrace();
    }
    }

/*

package batingaanimalmedicalcenterpetappointmentsystem;

import java.sql.Connection;
import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import java.sql.SQLException;


public class Homepage extends javax.swing.JFrame {

    private int currentUserId;

    public Homepage(int userId) {
        initComponents();
        checkUserProfile();
        this.currentUserId = userId;

        jLabel6.setText("Welcome, " + UserSession.getUsername() + "!");

        loadDashboardData();
    }
    
private void testDatabaseConnection() {
        Connection conn = DBConnection.getConnection();

        if (conn != null) {
            javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                this,
                "Database connected successfully!",
                "Database Test",
                javax.swing.JOptionPane.INFORMATION_MESSAGE
            );

            try {
                conn.close();
            } catch (SQLException e) {
                e.printStackTrace();
            }
        } else {
            javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                this,
                "Database connection failed!",
                "Database Test",
                javax.swing.JOptionPane.ERROR_MESSAGE
            );
        }
    }

 private void loadDashboardData() {
// =========================
    // 1. PETS REGISTERED
    // =========================
    String sqlPets = "SELECT COUNT(*) AS total FROM tbl_pet WHERE user_id = ?";

    try (Connection conn = DBConnection.getConnection();
         PreparedStatement pst = conn.prepareStatement(sqlPets)) {

        pst.setInt(1, currentUserId);
        ResultSet rs = pst.executeQuery();

        if (rs.next()) {
            lblPetsRegisteredCount.setText(String.valueOf(rs.getInt("total")));
        }

    } catch (SQLException e) {
        lblPetsRegisteredCount.setText("0");
        e.printStackTrace();
    }

    // =========================
    // 2. PENDING REQUESTS
    // =========================
    String sqlPending = "SELECT COUNT(*) AS total FROM tbl_appointments a " +
                        "JOIN tbl_pet p ON a.pet_id = p.pet_id " +
                        "WHERE p.user_id = ? AND a.status = 'Pending'";

    try (Connection conn = DBConnection.getConnection();
         PreparedStatement pst = conn.prepareStatement(sqlPending)) {

        pst.setInt(1, currentUserId);
        ResultSet rs = pst.executeQuery();

        if (rs.next()) {
            lblPendingRequestsCount.setText(String.valueOf(rs.getInt("total")));
        }

    } catch (SQLException e) {
        lblPendingRequestsCount.setText("0");
        e.printStackTrace();
    }

    // =========================
    // 3. PAST APPOINTMENTS
    // =========================
    String sqlPast = "SELECT COUNT(*) AS total FROM tbl_appointments a " +
                     "JOIN tbl_pet p ON a.pet_id = p.pet_id " +
                     "WHERE p.user_id = ? AND a.appointment_date < CURDATE()";

    try (Connection conn = DBConnection.getConnection();
         PreparedStatement pst = conn.prepareStatement(sqlPast)) {

        pst.setInt(1, currentUserId);
        ResultSet rs = pst.executeQuery();

        if (rs.next()) {
            lblPastAppointmentsCount.setText(String.valueOf(rs.getInt("total")));
        }

    } catch (SQLException e) {
        lblPastAppointmentsCount.setText("0");
        e.printStackTrace();
    }

    // =========================
    // 4. UPCOMING APPOINTMENTS
    // =========================
    String sqlUpcoming = "SELECT COUNT(*) AS total FROM tbl_appointments a " +
                         "JOIN tbl_pet p ON a.pet_id = p.pet_id " +
                         "WHERE p.user_id = ? AND a.status = 'Approved' AND a.appointment_date >= CURDATE()";

    try (Connection conn = DBConnection.getConnection();
         PreparedStatement pst = conn.prepareStatement(sqlUpcoming)) {

        pst.setInt(1, currentUserId);
        ResultSet rs = pst.executeQuery();

        if (rs.next()) {
            lblUpcomingAppointmentsCount.setText(String.valueOf(rs.getInt("total")));
        }

    } catch (SQLException e) {
        lblUpcomingAppointmentsCount.setText("0");
        e.printStackTrace();
    }
    
}

 private void checkUserProfile() {
 
    String query = "SELECT fullname, contact_number, address FROM tbl_users WHERE user_id = ?";
    
    try (Connection conn = DBConnection.getConnection();
         PreparedStatement pstmt = conn.prepareStatement(query)) {
        
        pstmt.setInt(1, UserSession.getUserId());
        try (ResultSet rs = pstmt.executeQuery()) {
            if (rs.next()) {
                String fullName = rs.getString("fullname");
                String contact = rs.getString("contact_number");
                String address = rs.getString("address");
                
                // Check if any required profile field is missing or empty
                if (fullName == null || fullName.trim().isEmpty() || 
                    contact == null || contact.trim().isEmpty() || 
                    address == null || address.trim().isEmpty()) {
                    
                    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                        this, 
                        "Please complete your profile information (Full Name, Contact Number, and Address) before proceeding.", 
                        "Profile Incomplete", 
                        javax.swing.JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE
                    );
                    
                    // Automatically open the My Profile screen
                    new MyProfile().setVisible(true);
                    this.dispose(); // Close current window
                }
            }
        }
    } catch (Exception e) {
        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Error checking profile: " + e.getMessage());
    }
}

    private void btnLogoutActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                          
       int choice = javax.swing.JOptionPane.showConfirmDialog(
        this,
        "Are you sure you want to logout?",
        "Logout Confirmation",
        javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_NO_OPTION,
        javax.swing.JOptionPane.QUESTION_MESSAGE
    );

    if (choice == javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_OPTION) {

        UserSession.clearSession();

        loginform login = new loginform();
        login.setVisible(true);
        this.dispose();
    }
    }

    private void jButton1ActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                         
     AddPetsScreen add = new AddPetsScreen();

     this.dispose();

MyPets

package batingaanimalmedicalcenterpetappointmentsystem;

import java.sql.Connection;
import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.sql.DriverManager;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.LocalTime;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import javax.swing.JOptionPane;
import javax.swing.table.DefaultTableModel;




/**
 *
 * @author arian
 */
public class MyPets extends javax.swing.JFrame {
    
    private static final java.util.logging.Logger logger = java.util.logging.Logger.getLogger(MyPets.class.getName());

    /**
     * Creates new form MyPets
     */
public MyPets() {
    initComponents();
    
    // 1. Check the session first
    if (UserSession.getUserId() == 0) {
        JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Please log in first.",
            "Login Required",
            JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE
        );
        
        new loginform().setVisible(true);
        this.dispose();
        return; // Stop execution here so it doesn't try to load pets
    }
    loadUserPets(UserSession.getUserId());
}
    
    // =====================================================
// LOAD ALL PETS OF CURRENT USER
// =====================================================    
public void loadUserPets(int loggedInUserId) {
    // 1. Add "Owner" to your column names array
    String[] columnNames = {"ID", "Pet Name", "Allergies", "Pet Type", "Breed", "Sex", "Age", "Weight", "Owner"};
    DefaultTableModel model = new DefaultTableModel(columnNames, 0);
     
    String url = "jdbc:mysql://localhost:3306/batinganimalmedicalcenter";
    String user = "root";
    String password = "";
     
    // 2. Update query to JOIN tbl_users and select the owner's fullname
    String query = "SELECT p.pet_id, p.pet_name, p.allergies, p.pet_type, p.breed, p.sex, p.age, p.weight, u.fullname AS owner_name " +
                   "FROM tbl_pet p " +
                   "JOIN tbl_users u ON p.user_id = u.user_id " +
                   "WHERE p.user_id = ?";
     
    try (Connection conn = DriverManager.getConnection(url, user, password);
         PreparedStatement pstmt = conn.prepareStatement(query)) {
         
        pstmt.setInt(1, loggedInUserId);
         
        try (ResultSet rs = pstmt.executeQuery()) {
            while (rs.next()) {
                int petId = rs.getInt("pet_id");
                String petName = rs.getString("pet_name");
                String allergies = rs.getString("allergies");
                String petType = rs.getString("pet_type");
                String breed = rs.getString("breed");
                String sex = rs.getString("sex");
                String age = rs.getString("age");
                String weight = rs.getString("weight");
                String ownerName = rs.getString("owner_name"); // Retrieve owner name
                 
                // 3. Include ownerName in the row data object matching the new column order
                model.addRow(new Object[]{petId, petName, allergies, petType, breed, sex, age, weight, ownerName});
            }
             
            jTable1.setModel(model);
             
            // 4. Keep the ID column (index 0) hidden from the user view
            jTable1.getColumnModel().getColumn(0).setMinWidth(0);
            jTable1.getColumnModel().getColumn(0).setMaxWidth(0);
            jTable1.getColumnModel().getColumn(0).setWidth(0);
             
        }
    } catch (Exception e) {
        JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Error connecting to database: " + e.getMessage());
    }
}

private void loadPetsWithOwners() {
    DefaultTableModel model = (DefaultTableModel) jTable1.getModel();
    model.setRowCount(0);
    
    String query = "SELECT p.pet_id, p.pet_name, p.species, p.breed, u.fullname AS owner_name, u.contact_number " +
                   "FROM tbl_pet p " +
                   "JOIN tbl_users u ON p.user_id = u.user_id";
    
    try (Connection conn = DBConnection.getConnection();
         PreparedStatement pstmt = conn.prepareStatement(query);
         ResultSet rs = pstmt.executeQuery()) {
        
        while (rs.next()) {
            int petId = rs.getInt("pet_id");
            String petName = rs.getString("pet_name");
            String species = rs.getString("species");
            String breed = rs.getString("breed");
            String ownerName = rs.getString("owner_name");
            String contact = rs.getString("contact_number");
            
            // Add row to your table (make sure your table has columns for Owner Name and Contact!)
            model.addRow(new Object[]{petId, petName, species, breed, ownerName, contact});
        }
        
    } catch (Exception e) {
        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Error loading pet owners: " + e.getMessage());
    }
}

    private void btnLogoutActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                          
       int choice = javax.swing.JOptionPane.showConfirmDialog(
        this,
        "Are you sure you want to logout?",
        "Logout Confirmation",
        javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_NO_OPTION,
        javax.swing.JOptionPane.QUESTION_MESSAGE
    );

    if (choice == javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_OPTION) {
        UserSession.clearSession();

        loginform login = new loginform();
        login.setVisible(true);
        this.dispose();
    }
    
    }

    private void btnDashboard3ActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                              
    int selectedRow = jTable1.getSelectedRow();
    
    // Validation: Check if a row is selected
    if (selectedRow == -1) {
        JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Please select a pet from the table to edit.", "Selection Required", JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE);
        return;
    }
    
    // Get the pet_id from the hidden first column
    int petId = Integer.parseInt(jTable1.getValueAt(selectedRow, 0).toString());
    
    // Open the edit screen and pass the petId
    new editscreen(petId).setVisible(true);
    this.dispose();

    }

    public AddPetsScreen() {
        initComponents();
        if (UserSession.getUserId() == 0) {

        JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                this,
                "Please log in first.",
                "Login Required",
                JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE
        );

        new loginform().setVisible(true);
        this.dispose();
        return;
    }

    setupPetType();
    }
    
    private void setupPetType() {

    cmbPetType.setModel(
            new javax.swing.DefaultComboBoxModel<>(
                    new String[]{
                        "Dog",
                        "Cat",
                        "Bird",
                        "Rabbit",
                        "Hamster",
                        "Other"
                    }
            )
    );

    cmbPetType.setSelectedIndex(0);

    cmbBreed.setModel(
            new javax.swing.DefaultComboBoxModel<>(
                    new String[]{
                        "Mixed Breed",
                        "Labrador Retriever",
                        "Golden Retriever",
                        "German Shepherd",
                        "Shih Tzu",
                        "Pomeranian",
                        "Chihuahua",
                        "Poodle",
                        "Persian",
                      "Siamese",
                        "Other"
                    }
            )
    );

    cmbBreed.setSelectedIndex(0);  
}

    private void btnSavePetActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                           
   String petName = txtPetName.getText().trim();
    String allergies = txtAllergies.getText().trim(); // Get allergies input
    String age = txtAge.getText().trim();
    String weight = txtWeight.getText().trim();
    
    // 2. Get selected dropdown values safely
    String petType = (cmbPetType.getSelectedItem() != null) ? cmbPetType.getSelectedItem().toString() : "";
    String breed = (cmbBreed.getSelectedItem() != null) ? cmbBreed.getSelectedItem().toString() : "";
    String sex = (Petsex.getSelectedItem() != null) ? Petsex.getSelectedItem().toString() : "";
    
    int userId = UserSession.getUserId();

    // 3. Validation for text fields and combo boxes
    if (petName.isEmpty() || age.isEmpty() || weight.isEmpty()) {
        JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Please fill in all text fields.", "Validation Error", JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE);
        return;
    }
    
    // If the user left allergies blank, default it to "None"
    if (allergies.isEmpty()) {
        allergies = "None";
    }
    
    // Check dropdown selections
    if (petType.equals("") || petType.contains("Select")) {
        JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Please select a valid Pet Type.", "Validation Error", JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE);
        return;
    }
    
    if (breed.equals("") || breed.contains("Select")) {
        JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Please select a valid Breed.", "Validation Error", JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE);
        return;
    }
    
    if (sex.equals("") || sex.contains("Select")) {
        JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Please select a valid Sex.", "Validation Error", JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE);
        return;
    }

    // 4. Database Connection details & Insert Query including allergies
    String url = "jdbc:mysql://localhost:3306/batinganimalmedicalcenter";
    String dbUser = "root";
    String dbPassword = "";

    String query = "INSERT INTO tbl_pet (user_id, pet_name, allergies, pet_type, breed, sex, age, weight) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)";

    try (Connection conn = DriverManager.getConnection(url, dbUser, dbPassword);
         PreparedStatement pstmt = conn.prepareStatement(query)) {
        
        pstmt.setInt(1, userId);
        pstmt.setString(2, petName);
        pstmt.setString(3, allergies); // Insert allergies value
        pstmt.setString(4, petType);
        pstmt.setString(5, breed);
        pstmt.setString(6, sex);
        pstmt.setString(7, age);
        pstmt.setString(8, weight);
        
        pstmt.executeUpdate();
        
        JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Pet added successfully!");
        
        new MyPets().setVisible(true);
        this.dispose();
        
    } catch (Exception e) {
        JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Database Error: " + e.getMessage(), "Error", JOptionPane.ERROR_MESSAGE);
    }
    }

    private void btnLogoutActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                          
        int choice = javax.swing.JOptionPane.showConfirmDialog(
            this,
            "Are you sure you want to logout?",
            "Logout Confirmation",
            javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_NO_OPTION,
            javax.swing.JOptionPane.QUESTION_MESSAGE
        );

        if (choice == javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_OPTION) {

            UserSession.clearSession();

            loginform login = new loginform();
            login.setVisible(true);
            this.dispose();
        }
    }

EditsScreen

package batingaanimalmedicalcenterpetappointmentsystem;

import java.sql.Connection;
import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import javax.swing.JOptionPane;
import java.sql.DriverManager;


public class editscreen extends javax.swing.JFrame {
    
    private static final java.util.logging.Logger logger = java.util.logging.Logger.getLogger(editscreen.class.getName());

private int currentPetId;

    public editscreen() {
        initComponents();
    }

public editscreen(int petId) {
    initComponents();
    this.currentPetId = petId;
    loadPetDataForEditing(petId);
}

private void loadPetDataForEditing(int petId) {
    String url = "jdbc:mysql://localhost:3306/batinganimalmedicalcenter";
    String dbUser = "root";
    String dbPassword = "";
    
    String query = "SELECT pet_name, allergies, pet_type, weight, breed, age, sex FROM tbl_pet WHERE pet_id = ?";
    
    try (Connection conn = DriverManager.getConnection(url, dbUser, dbPassword);
         PreparedStatement pstmt = conn.prepareStatement(query)) {
        
        pstmt.setInt(1, petId);
        
        try (ResultSet rs = pstmt.executeQuery()) {
            if (rs.next()) {
                // Populate text fields
                txtPetName.setText(rs.getString("pet_name"));
                txtAllergies.setText(rs.getString("allergies"));
                txtAge.setText(rs.getString("age"));
                txtWeight.setText(rs.getString("weight"));
                
                // Populate combo boxes
                cmbPetType.setSelectedItem(rs.getString("pet_type"));
                cmbBreed.setSelectedItem(rs.getString("breed"));
                Petsex.setSelectedItem(rs.getString("sex"));
            }
        }
    } catch (Exception e) {
        JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Database Error: " + e.getMessage(), "Error", JOptionPane.ERROR_MESSAGE);
    }
}

    private void btnUpdatePetActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                             
      // 1. Get updated text values
    String petName = txtPetName.getText().trim();
    String allergies = txtAllergies.getText().trim();
    String age = txtAge.getText().trim();
    String weight = txtWeight.getText().trim();
    
    // 2. Get updated dropdown choices safely
    String petType = (cmbPetType.getSelectedItem() != null) ? cmbPetType.getSelectedItem().toString() : "";
    String breed = (cmbBreed.getSelectedItem() != null) ? cmbBreed.getSelectedItem().toString() : "";
    String sex = (Petsex.getSelectedItem() != null) ? Petsex.getSelectedItem().toString() : "";
    
    // 3. Basic validation
    if (petName.isEmpty() || age.isEmpty() || weight.isEmpty()) {
        JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Please fill in all text fields.", "Validation Error", JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE);
        return;
    }
    
    if (allergies.isEmpty()) {
        allergies = "None";
    }
    
    if (petType.equals("") || petType.contains("Select")) {
        JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Please select a valid Pet Type.", "Validation Error", JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE);
        return;
    }
    
    if (breed.equals("") || breed.contains("Select")) {
        JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Please select a valid Breed.", "Validation Error", JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE);
        return;
    }
    
    if (sex.equals("") || sex.contains("Select")) {
        JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Please select a valid Sex.", "Validation Error", JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE);
        return;
    }

    // 4. Database Connection details & UPDATE Query
    String url = "jdbc:mysql://localhost:3306/batinganimalmedicalcenter";
    String dbUser = "root";
    String dbPassword = "";

    // SQL Update statement targeting the specific currentPetId
    String query = "UPDATE tbl_pet SET pet_name = ?, allergies = ?, pet_type = ?, weight = ?, breed = ?, age = ?, sex = ? WHERE pet_id = ?";

    try (Connection conn = DriverManager.getConnection(url, dbUser, dbPassword);
         PreparedStatement pstmt = conn.prepareStatement(query)) {
        
        pstmt.setString(1, petName);
        pstmt.setString(2, allergies);
        pstmt.setString(3, petType);
        pstmt.setString(4, weight);
        pstmt.setString(5, breed);
        pstmt.setString(6, age);
        pstmt.setString(7, sex);
        pstmt.setInt(8, currentPetId); // Uses the ID stored when the screen opened
        
        pstmt.executeUpdate();
        
        JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Pet updated successfully!");
        
        // Return back to the MyPets screen and close this edit window
        new MyPets().setVisible(true);
        this.dispose();
        
    } catch (Exception e) {
        JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Database Error: " + e.getMessage(), "Error", JOptionPane.ERROR_MESSAGE);
    }
    }

    private void btnLogoutActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                          
        int choice = javax.swing.JOptionPane.showConfirmDialog(
            this,
            "Are you sure you want to logout?",
            "Logout Confirmation",
            javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_NO_OPTION,
            javax.swing.JOptionPane.QUESTION_MESSAGE
        );

        if (choice == javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_OPTION) {

            UserSession.clearSession();

            loginform login = new loginform();
            login.setVisible(true);
            this.dispose();
        }
    }

BookAppointment

package batingaanimalmedicalcenterpetappointmentsystem;
import javax.swing.JOptionPane;
import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.Connection;
import java.sql.Date;
import java.sql.Time;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import java.sql.SQLException;
/**
 *
 * @author arian
 */
public class BookAppointment extends javax.swing.JFrame {
    
    
    private static final java.util.logging.Logger logger = java.util.logging.Logger.getLogger(BookAppointment.class.getName());

    /**
     * Creates new form BookAppointment
     */
    public BookAppointment() {
    initComponents();

    if (UserSession.getUserId() == 0) {

        JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                this,
                "Please log in first.",
                "Login Required",
                JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE
        );

        new loginform().setVisible(true);
        this.dispose();
        return;
    }

    loadPets();
    
    cmbService.setModel(new javax.swing.DefaultComboBoxModel<>(new String[] {
    "General Check-up",
    "Vaccination",
    "Deworming",
    "Grooming",
    "Dental Care",
    "Laboratory Test",
    "Surgery",
    "Emergency Consultation"
}));

cmbService.setEditable(false);
cmbSelectPet.setEditable(false);
}
    
    
    
    private void loadPets() {

    cmbSelectPet.removeAllItems();

    String sql =
            "SELECT pet_id, pet_name " +
            "FROM tbl_pet " +
            "WHERE user_id = ? " +
            "ORDER BY pet_name ASC";

    try (Connection conn = DBConnection.getConnection();
         PreparedStatement pst = conn.prepareStatement(sql)) {

        pst.setInt(1, UserSession.getUserId());

        ResultSet rs = pst.executeQuery();

        while (rs.next()) {

            cmbSelectPet.addItem(
                    rs.getInt("pet_id") + " - " + rs.getString("pet_name")
            );
        }

    } catch (SQLException e) {

        JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                this,
                "Unable to load your pets.\n" + e.getMessage(),
                "Database Error",
                JOptionPane.ERROR_MESSAGE
        );

        e.printStackTrace();
    }
}

    private void loadUserPetsIntoComboBox() {
    cmbSelectPet.removeAllItems(); // Replace with your actual combo box variable name if different
    
    String query = "SELECT pet_id, pet_name FROM tbl_pet WHERE user_id = ?";
    
    try (Connection conn = DBConnection.getConnection();
         PreparedStatement pstmt = conn.prepareStatement(query)) {
        
        pstmt.setInt(1, UserSession.getUserId());
        
        try (ResultSet rs = pstmt.executeQuery()) {
            while (rs.next()) {
                int petId = rs.getInt("pet_id");
                String petName = rs.getString("pet_name");
                
                // Format display as "ID - PetName" so we can easily parse the ID later
                cmbSelectPet.addItem(petId + " - " + petName);
            }
        }
    } catch (Exception e) {
        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Error loading pets: " + e.getMessage());
    }
}
    
    private double getServiceAmount(String service) {

    switch (service) {

        case "General Check-up":
            return 500;

        case "Vaccination":
            return 800;

        case "Deworming":
            return 600;

        case "Grooming":
            return 700;

        case "Dental Care":
            return 600;

        case "Laboratory Test":
            return 1000;

        case "Surgery":
            return 3000;

        case "Emergency Consultation":
            return 1500;

        default:
            return 0;
    }
}

    private void btnConfirmAppointmentActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                                      
// 1. Get selected pet item from ComboBox
String selectedItem = (String) cmbSelectPet.getSelectedItem();
    if (selectedItem == null || selectedItem.isEmpty()) {
        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Please select a pet.", "Warning", javax.swing.JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE);
        return;
    }
    
    int petId = Integer.parseInt(selectedItem.split(" - ")[0]);
    
    // 2. Get form values
    String serviceType = (String) cmbService.getSelectedItem();
    String appointmentTime = (String) cmbTime.getSelectedItem();
    
    // Get date from JDateChooser
    java.util.Date selectedDate = jDateChooser1.getDate();
    if (selectedDate == null) {
        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Please select an appointment date.", "Warning", javax.swing.JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE);
        return;
    }
    
    java.text.SimpleDateFormat dateFormat = new java.text.SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd");
    String appointmentDate = dateFormat.format(selectedDate);
    
    // 3. Get and validate Reason for Visit
    String reasonForVisit = Reason.getText().trim(); // Adjust variable name if different
    if (reasonForVisit.isEmpty()) {
        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Please enter a reason for the visit.", "Warning", javax.swing.JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE);
        Reason.requestFocus(); // Focuses cursor on the text box
        return;
    }
    
    // 4. Get amount using your service method
    double amount = getServiceAmount(serviceType);
    
    // 5. Check if time slot is occupied
    String checkQuery = "SELECT COUNT(*) AS booked FROM tbl_appointments WHERE appointment_date = ? AND appointment_time = ?";
    
    try (Connection conn = DBConnection.getConnection();
         PreparedStatement checkStmt = conn.prepareStatement(checkQuery)) {
        
        checkStmt.setString(1, appointmentDate);
        checkStmt.setString(2, appointmentTime);
        
        try (ResultSet rs = checkStmt.executeQuery()) {
            if (rs.next() && rs.getInt("booked") > 0) {
                javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                    this, 
                    "Sorry! The time slot " + appointmentTime + " on " + appointmentDate + " is already occupied.", 
                    "Time Slot Occupied", 
                    javax.swing.JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE
                );
                return;
            }
        }
    } catch (Exception e) {
        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Database Error: " + e.getMessage());
        return;
    }
    
    // 6. Insert into tbl_appointments including the reason_for_visit
    String insertQuery = "INSERT INTO tbl_appointments (pet_id, service_type, appointment_date, appointment_time, amount, status, reason_for_visit) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)";
    
    try (Connection conn = DBConnection.getConnection();
         PreparedStatement pstmt = conn.prepareStatement(insertQuery)) {
        
        pstmt.setInt(1, petId);
        pstmt.setString(2, serviceType);
        pstmt.setString(3, appointmentDate);
        pstmt.setString(4, appointmentTime);
        pstmt.setDouble(5, amount);
        pstmt.setString(6, "Pending");
        pstmt.setString(7, reasonForVisit);
        
        int rowsInserted = pstmt.executeUpdate();
        if (rowsInserted > 0) {
            javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Appointment booked successfully!");
            
            new Homepage(UserSession.getUserId()).setVisible(true);
            this.dispose();
        }
        
    } catch (Exception e) {
        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Database Error: " + e.getMessage());
        e.printStackTrace();
    }
    }

package batingaanimalmedicalcenterpetappointmentsystem;
import java.sql.Connection;
import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import javax.swing.table.DefaultTableModel;
import javax.swing.JOptionPane;
public class Myappointment extends javax.swing.JFrame {
    
    private static final java.util.logging.Logger logger = java.util.logging.Logger.getLogger(Myappointment.class.getName());

     * Creates new form Myappointment
     */
    public Myappointment() {
        initComponents();
        loadAppointments();
        setupCancelAction();
    }
    
    
    
   private void loadAppointments() {
DefaultTableModel model = (DefaultTableModel) tblMyAppointments.getModel();
    
    // Explicitly set the 7 column headers so they never mismatch!
    model.setColumnIdentifiers(new String[]{
        "ID", "Pet Name", "Service", "Date", "Time", "Status", "Action"
    });
    
    model.setRowCount(0); // Clear old rows
    
    String sql = "SELECT a.appointment_id, p.pet_name, a.service_type, a.appointment_date, a.appointment_time, a.amount, a.status " +
                 "FROM tbl_appointments a " +
                 "JOIN tbl_pet p ON a.pet_id = p.pet_id " +
                 "WHERE p.user_id = ? " +
                 "ORDER BY a.appointment_date ASC, a.appointment_time ASC";
                 
    double totalExpenses = 0.0;
    
    try (Connection con = DBConnection.getConnection();
         PreparedStatement pst = con.prepareStatement(sql)) {
        
        pst.setInt(1, UserSession.getUserId());
        
        try (ResultSet rs = pst.executeQuery()) {
            while (rs.next()) {
                int appointmentId = rs.getInt("appointment_id");
                String petName = rs.getString("pet_name");
                String serviceType = rs.getString("service_type");
                String appointmentDate = rs.getString("appointment_date");
                String appointmentTime = rs.getString("appointment_time");
                double amount = rs.getDouble("amount");
                String status = rs.getString("status");
                
                totalExpenses += amount;
                
                String actionText = "Cancel";
                if (status != null && (status.equalsIgnoreCase("Completed") || status.equalsIgnoreCase("Approved") || status.equalsIgnoreCase("Cancelled"))) {
                    actionText = ""; 
                }
                
                model.addRow(new Object[]{
                    appointmentId, 
                    petName, 
                    serviceType, 
                    appointmentDate, 
                    appointmentTime, 
                    status, 
                    actionText
                });
            }
            
            lblTotalExpenses.setText("Total Expenses: ₱ " + String.format("%.2f", totalExpenses));
            
            // Hide the ID column (index 0) safely
            if (tblMyAppointments.getColumnCount() > 0) {
                tblMyAppointments.getColumnModel().getColumn(0).setMinWidth(0);
                tblMyAppointments.getColumnModel().getColumn(0).setMaxWidth(0);
                tblMyAppointments.getColumnModel().getColumn(0).setWidth(0);
            }
            tblMyAppointments.setRowHeight(24);
        }
} catch (Exception e) {
    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Unable to load appointments: " + e.getMessage());
    e.printStackTrace();
}
}
    private void setupCancelAction() {
tblMyAppointments.addMouseListener(
        new java.awt.event.MouseAdapter() {

            @Override
            public void mouseClicked(java.awt.event.MouseEvent evt) {
                int row = tblMyAppointments.rowAtPoint(evt.getPoint());
                int column = tblMyAppointments.columnAtPoint(evt.getPoint());

                // Column 6 = Action
                if (column != 6 || row < 0) {
                    return;
                }

                // Get the action text of the clicked cell
                Object actionVal = tblMyAppointments.getValueAt(row, 6);
                if (actionVal == null || !actionVal.toString().equals("Cancel")) {
                    return; // Do nothing if the cell is blank (for Completed/Approved items)
                }

                int choice = javax.swing.JOptionPane.showConfirmDialog(
                        Myappointment.this,
                        "Are you sure you want to cancel this appointment?",
                        "Cancel Appointment",
                        javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_NO_OPTION,
                        javax.swing.JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE
                );

                if (choice != javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_OPTION) {
                    return;
                }

                String appointmentId = tblMyAppointments.getValueAt(row, 0).toString();

                // Note: If appointment_id is a pure number in tbl_appointments, parse it directly:
                int aptId = Integer.parseInt(appointmentId);

                // Update the actual appointment table status to 'Cancelled' instead of clearing tbl_pet
                String sql = "UPDATE tbl_appointments SET status = 'Cancelled' WHERE appointment_id = ?";

                try (Connection con = DBConnection.getConnection();
                     PreparedStatement pst = con.prepareStatement(sql)) {

                    pst.setInt(1, aptId);
                    int result = pst.executeUpdate();

                    if (result > 0) {
                        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                                Myappointment.this,
                                "Appointment cancelled successfully!",
                                "Success",
                                javax.swing.JOptionPane.INFORMATION_MESSAGE
                        );
                        loadAppointments();
                    } else {
                        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                                Myappointment.this,
                                "Appointment could not be cancelled.",
                                "Error",
                                javax.swing.JOptionPane.ERROR_MESSAGE
                        );
                    }

                } catch (Exception e) {
                    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                            Myappointment.this,
                            "Database Error: " + e.getMessage(),
                            "Error",
                            javax.swing.JOptionPane.ERROR_MESSAGE
                    );
                    e.printStackTrace();
                }
            }
        }
    );
}

    private void btnLogoutActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                          
        int choice = javax.swing.JOptionPane.showConfirmDialog(
            this,
            "Are you sure you want to logout?",
            "Logout Confirmation",
            javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_NO_OPTION,
            javax.swing.JOptionPane.QUESTION_MESSAGE
        );

        if (choice == javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_OPTION) {

            UserSession.clearSession();

            loginform login = new loginform();
            login.setVisible(true);
            this.dispose();
        }
    }

/*

package batingaanimalmedicalcenterpetappointmentsystem;
import java.sql.Connection;
import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import javax.swing.table.DefaultTableModel;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.LocalTime;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;

public class Petrecords extends javax.swing.JFrame {
    
    private static final java.util.logging.Logger logger = java.util.logging.Logger.getLogger(Petrecords.class.getName());

    /**
     * Creates new form Petrecords
     */
 public Petrecords() {
    initComponents();
    loadUserPets();
    
    // Reload the table whenever a different pet is selected from the dropdown
    cmbSelectPet.addActionListener(e -> loadPetMedicalHistory());
    
    // Load records for the initially selected pet on open
    loadPetMedicalHistory();
  
}
 
 private void loadUserPets() {
    cmbSelectPet.removeAllItems();
    String query = "SELECT pet_id, pet_name FROM tbl_pet WHERE user_id = ?";
    
    try (Connection conn = DBConnection.getConnection();
         PreparedStatement pstmt = conn.prepareStatement(query)) {
        
        pstmt.setInt(1, UserSession.getUserId());
        try (ResultSet rs = pstmt.executeQuery()) {
            while (rs.next()) {
                int petId = rs.getInt("pet_id");
                String petName = rs.getString("pet_name");
                cmbSelectPet.addItem(petId + " - " + petName);
            }
        }
    } catch (Exception e) {
        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Error loading pets: " + e.getMessage());
    }
}
 
 private void loadPetMedicalHistory() {
    String selectedItem = (String) cmbSelectPet.getSelectedItem();
    if (selectedItem == null || selectedItem.isEmpty()) {
        return;
    }
    
    // Extract pet_id from the dropdown string (e.g., "22 - Clintoy" -> 22)
    int petId = Integer.parseInt(selectedItem.split(" - ")[0]);
    
    DefaultTableModel model = (DefaultTableModel) tblMedicalHistory.getModel();
    model.setRowCount(0); // Clear old table rows
    
    // Query matching your exact columns in phpMyAdmin
    String query = "SELECT appointment_date, diagnosis, treatment, veterinarian FROM tbl_medical_history WHERE pet_id = ? ORDER BY appointment_date DESC";
    
    try (Connection conn = DBConnection.getConnection();
         PreparedStatement pstmt = conn.prepareStatement(query)) {
        
        pstmt.setInt(1, petId);
        try (ResultSet rs = pstmt.executeQuery()) {
            while (rs.next()) {
                String date = rs.getString("appointment_date");
                String diagnosis = rs.getString("diagnosis");
                String treatment = rs.getString("treatment");
                String vet = rs.getString("veterinarian");
                
                // Adds row matching your table view: Date, Diagnosis, Treatment, Veterinarian
                model.addRow(new Object[]{date, diagnosis, treatment, vet});
            }
        }
    } catch (Exception e) {
        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Error loading pet records: " + e.getMessage());
        e.printStackTrace();
    }
}

    private void cmbSelectPetActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                             
        String selectedItem = cmbSelectPet.getSelectedItem().toString();
    String petId = selectedItem.split(" - ")[0].trim(); // Gets the number before the dash

    // Clear existing table rows
    javax.swing.table.DefaultTableModel model = (javax.swing.table.DefaultTableModel) tblMedicalHistory.getModel();
    model.setRowCount(0);

    // Query tbl_visit_records for this pet
    String sql = "SELECT visit_date, diagnosis, treatment, veterinarian FROM tbl_visit_records WHERE pet_id = ?";

    try (java.sql.Connection con = DBConnection.getConnection();
         java.sql.PreparedStatement pst = con.prepareStatement(sql)) {
        
        pst.setString(1, petId);
        try (java.sql.ResultSet rs = pst.executeQuery()) {
            while (rs.next()) {
                String date = rs.getString("visit_date");
                String diagnosis = rs.getString("diagnosis");
                String treatment = rs.getString("treatment");
                String veterinarian = rs.getString("veterinarian");

                // Add row to your table
                model.addRow(new Object[]{date, diagnosis, treatment, veterinarian});
            }
        }
    } catch (Exception e) {
        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Error loading pet records: " + e.getMessage());
        e.printStackTrace();
    }
    }

    private void btnLogoutActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                          
        int choice = javax.swing.JOptionPane.showConfirmDialog(
            this,
            "Are you sure you want to logout?",
            "Logout Confirmation",
            javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_NO_OPTION,
            javax.swing.JOptionPane.QUESTION_MESSAGE
        );

        if (choice == javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_OPTION) {

            UserSession.clearSession();

            loginform login = new loginform();
            login.setVisible(true);
            this.dispose();
        }
    }

/*

package batingaanimalmedicalcenterpetappointmentsystem;
import java.sql.Connection;
import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import javax.swing.table.DefaultTableModel;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.LocalTime;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;

public class Petrecords extends javax.swing.JFrame {
    
    private static final java.util.logging.Logger logger = java.util.logging.Logger.getLogger(Petrecords.class.getName());

    /**
     * Creates new form Petrecords
     */
 public Petrecords() {
    initComponents();
    loadUserPets();
    
    // Reload the table whenever a different pet is selected from the dropdown
    cmbSelectPet.addActionListener(e -> loadPetMedicalHistory());
    
    // Load records for the initially selected pet on open
    loadPetMedicalHistory();
  
}
 
 private void loadUserPets() {
    cmbSelectPet.removeAllItems();
    String query = "SELECT pet_id, pet_name FROM tbl_pet WHERE user_id = ?";
    
    try (Connection conn = DBConnection.getConnection();
         PreparedStatement pstmt = conn.prepareStatement(query)) {
        
        pstmt.setInt(1, UserSession.getUserId());
        try (ResultSet rs = pstmt.executeQuery()) {
            while (rs.next()) {
                int petId = rs.getInt("pet_id");
                String petName = rs.getString("pet_name");
                cmbSelectPet.addItem(petId + " - " + petName);
            }
        }
    } catch (Exception e) {
        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Error loading pets: " + e.getMessage());
    }
}
 
 private void loadPetMedicalHistory() {
    String selectedItem = (String) cmbSelectPet.getSelectedItem();
    if (selectedItem == null || selectedItem.isEmpty()) {
        return;
    }
    
    // Extract pet_id from the dropdown string (e.g., "22 - Clintoy" -> 22)
    int petId = Integer.parseInt(selectedItem.split(" - ")[0]);
    
    DefaultTableModel model = (DefaultTableModel) tblMedicalHistory.getModel();
    model.setRowCount(0); // Clear old table rows
    
    // Query matching your exact columns in phpMyAdmin
    String query = "SELECT appointment_date, diagnosis, treatment, veterinarian FROM tbl_medical_history WHERE pet_id = ? ORDER BY appointment_date DESC";
    
    try (Connection conn = DBConnection.getConnection();
         PreparedStatement pstmt = conn.prepareStatement(query)) {
        
        pstmt.setInt(1, petId);
        try (ResultSet rs = pstmt.executeQuery()) {
            while (rs.next()) {
                String date = rs.getString("appointment_date");
                String diagnosis = rs.getString("diagnosis");
                String treatment = rs.getString("treatment");
                String vet = rs.getString("veterinarian");
                
                // Adds row matching your table view: Date, Diagnosis, Treatment, Veterinarian
                model.addRow(new Object[]{date, diagnosis, treatment, vet});
            }
        }
    } catch (Exception e) {
        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Error loading pet records: " + e.getMessage());
        e.printStackTrace();
    }
}

    private void cmbSelectPetActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                             
        String selectedItem = cmbSelectPet.getSelectedItem().toString();
    String petId = selectedItem.split(" - ")[0].trim(); // Gets the number before the dash

    // Clear existing table rows
    javax.swing.table.DefaultTableModel model = (javax.swing.table.DefaultTableModel) tblMedicalHistory.getModel();
    model.setRowCount(0);

    // Query tbl_visit_records for this pet
    String sql = "SELECT visit_date, diagnosis, treatment, veterinarian FROM tbl_visit_records WHERE pet_id = ?";

    try (java.sql.Connection con = DBConnection.getConnection();
         java.sql.PreparedStatement pst = con.prepareStatement(sql)) {
        
        pst.setString(1, petId);
        try (java.sql.ResultSet rs = pst.executeQuery()) {
            while (rs.next()) {
                String date = rs.getString("visit_date");
                String diagnosis = rs.getString("diagnosis");
                String treatment = rs.getString("treatment");
                String veterinarian = rs.getString("veterinarian");

                // Add row to your table
                model.addRow(new Object[]{date, diagnosis, treatment, veterinarian});
            }
        }
    } catch (Exception e) {
        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Error loading pet records: " + e.getMessage());
        e.printStackTrace();
    }
    }

    private void btnLogoutActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                          
        int choice = javax.swing.JOptionPane.showConfirmDialog(
            this,
            "Are you sure you want to logout?",
            "Logout Confirmation",
            javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_NO_OPTION,
            javax.swing.JOptionPane.QUESTION_MESSAGE
        );

        if (choice == javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_OPTION) {

            UserSession.clearSession();

            loginform login = new loginform();
            login.setVisible(true);
            this.dispose();
        }
    }

MyProfile

package batingaanimalmedicalcenterpetappointmentsystem;

import java.sql.Connection;
import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import javax.swing.table.DefaultTableModel;

public class MyProfile extends javax.swing.JFrame {
    
    private static final java.util.logging.Logger logger = java.util.logging.Logger.getLogger(MyProfile.class.getName());

    /**
     * Creates new form Profile
     */
    public MyProfile() {
        initComponents();
        loadUserProfile();
        if (UserSession.getUserId() == 0) {
        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                this,
                "Please log in first.",
                "Login Required",
                javax.swing.JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE
        );

        new loginform().setVisible(true);
        this.dispose();
        return;
    }
    }
 
private void loadUserProfile() {
    String query = "SELECT fullname, username, email, contact_number, address FROM tbl_users WHERE user_id = ?";
    
    try (Connection conn = DBConnection.getConnection();
         PreparedStatement pstmt = conn.prepareStatement(query)) {
        
        pstmt.setInt(1, UserSession.getUserId());
        try (ResultSet rs = pstmt.executeQuery()) {
            if (rs.next()) {
                txtFullname.setText(rs.getString("fullname") != null ? rs.getString("fullname") : "");
                txtUsername.setText(rs.getString("username") != null ? rs.getString("username") : "");
                txtEmail.setText(rs.getString("email") != null ? rs.getString("email") : "");
                txtContactNumber.setText(rs.getString("contact_number") != null ? rs.getString("contact_number") : "");
                txtAddress.setText(rs.getString("address") != null ? rs.getString("address") : "");
            }
        }
    } catch (Exception e) {
        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Error loading profile: " + e.getMessage());
    }
    
}

private boolean checkProfileCompletion() {
    String sql = "SELECT full_name, contact_number, address FROM tbl_users WHERE user_id = ?";
    
    try (Connection con = DBConnection.getConnection();
         PreparedStatement pst = con.prepareStatement(sql)) {
        
        pst.setInt(1, UserSession.getUserId());
        
        try (ResultSet rs = pst.executeQuery()) {
            if (rs.next()) {
                String fullName = rs.getString("full_name");
                String contact = rs.getString("contact_number");
                String address = rs.getString("address");
                
                // Check if any required profile field is missing or empty
                if (fullName == null || fullName.trim().isEmpty() ||
                    contact == null || contact.trim().isEmpty() ||
                    address == null || address.trim().isEmpty()) {
                    
                    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                        this, 
                        "Please complete and save your profile information (Full Name, Contact Number, and Address) before accessing other pages.", 
                        "Profile Incomplete", 
                        javax.swing.JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE
                    );
                    return false; // Profile is NOT complete
                }
            }
        }
    } catch (Exception e) {
        e.printStackTrace();
    }
    return true; // Profile is complete
}

    private void btnSaveChangesActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                               
        String fullName = txtFullname.getText().trim();
    String email = txtEmail.getText().trim();
    String contact = txtContactNumber.getText().trim();
    String address = txtAddress.getText().trim();
    
    String query = "UPDATE tbl_users SET fullname = ?, email = ?, contact_number = ?, address = ? WHERE user_id = ?";
    
    try (Connection conn = DBConnection.getConnection();
         PreparedStatement pstmt = conn.prepareStatement(query)) {
        
        pstmt.setString(1, fullName);
        pstmt.setString(2, email);
        pstmt.setString(3, contact);
        pstmt.setString(4, address);
        pstmt.setInt(5, UserSession.getUserId());
        
        int rowsUpdated = pstmt.executeUpdate();
        if (rowsUpdated > 0) {
            javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Profile updated successfully!");
        }
        
    } catch (Exception e) {
        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Database Error: " + e.getMessage());
    }
    }

    private void btnLogoutActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                          
        int choice = javax.swing.JOptionPane.showConfirmDialog(
            this,
            "Are you sure you want to logout?",
            "Logout Confirmation",
            javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_NO_OPTION,
            javax.swing.JOptionPane.QUESTION_MESSAGE
        );

        if (choice == javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_OPTION) {

            UserSession.clearSession();

            loginform login = new loginform();
            login.setVisible(true);
            this.dispose();
        }
    }

    private void jButton2ActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                         
      loginform login = new loginform();
      login.setVisible(true);
      this.dispose();
    }                                        

    private void jButton3ActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                         
      
    String uN = username.getText().trim();
    String pW = new String(password.getPassword()).trim();

    if (uN.isEmpty() || pW.isEmpty()) {

        JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                this,
                "Please enter your username and password.",
                "Login",
                JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE
        );

        return;
    }

    try {

        Connection con = DBConnection.getConnection();

        String sql = "SELECT * FROM tbl_admin WHERE username=? AND password=?";

        PreparedStatement pst = con.prepareStatement(sql);

        pst.setString(1, uN);
        pst.setString(2, pW);

        ResultSet rs = pst.executeQuery();

        if (rs.next()) {

            int userID = rs.getInt("admin_id");

            UserSession.setUser(userID, uN);

            JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                    this,
                    "Login Successful!"
            );

           new Adminhome().setVisible(true);
            this.dispose();

        } else {

            JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                    this,
                    "Invalid username or password",
                    "Login",
                    JOptionPane.ERROR_MESSAGE
            );
        }

        con.close();

    } catch (Exception e) {

        JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                this,
                "Error: " + e.getMessage(),
                "Login Error",
                JOptionPane.ERROR_MESSAGE
        );

        e.printStackTrace();
    }
    }                                        

    private void jCheckBox1ActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                           
        if (jCheckBox1.isSelected()) {
            password.setEchoChar((char) 0);
            password.setEchoChar((char) 0);
        } else {
            password.setEchoChar('•');
            password.setEchoChar('*');
        }
    }

Adminhome

package batingaanimalmedicalcenterpetappointmentsystem;

import java.sql.Connection;
import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import javax.swing.table.DefaultTableModel;
import javax.swing.JOptionPane;

public class Adminhome extends javax.swing.JFrame {
    
    private static final java.util.logging.Logger logger = java.util.logging.Logger.getLogger(Adminhome.class.getName());

    /**
     * Creates new form Adminhome
     */
    public Adminhome() {
        initComponents();
        loadAdminDashboardData();
    }

private void loadAdminDashboardData() {
    // 1. Fetch counts for the stat boxes
    try (Connection conn = DBConnection.getConnection()) {
        
        // Today's Appointments Count
        String countTodaySql = "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tbl_appointments WHERE appointment_date = CURDATE()";
        try (PreparedStatement pst = conn.prepareStatement(countTodaySql);
             ResultSet rs = pst.executeQuery()) {
            if (rs.next()) {
                lblTodayCount.setText(String.valueOf(rs.getInt(1)));
            }
        }
        
        // Pending Appointments Count
        String countPendingSql = "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tbl_appointments WHERE status = 'Pending'";
        try (PreparedStatement pst = conn.prepareStatement(countPendingSql);
             ResultSet rs = pst.executeQuery()) {
            if (rs.next()) {
                lblPendingCount.setText(String.valueOf(rs.getInt(1)));
            }
        }
        
        // Completed Appointments Count
        String countCompletedSql = "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tbl_appointments WHERE status = 'Completed'";
        try (PreparedStatement pst = conn.prepareStatement(countCompletedSql);
             ResultSet rs = pst.executeQuery()) {
            if (rs.next()) {
                lblCompletedCount.setText(String.valueOf(rs.getInt(1)));
            }
        }
        
        // Total Pets Registered Count
        String countPetsSql = "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tbl_pet";
        try (PreparedStatement pst = conn.prepareStatement(countPetsSql);
             ResultSet rs = pst.executeQuery()) {
            if (rs.next()) {
                lblAllPetsCount.setText(String.valueOf(rs.getInt(1)));
            }
        }
        
    } catch (Exception e) {
        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Error loading statistics: " + e.getMessage());
    }
    
    // 2. Load Today's Appointments into the table
    DefaultTableModel model = (DefaultTableModel) jTable1.getModel(); // Replace with your table name if different
    model.setRowCount(0);
    
    String tableSql = "SELECT a.appointment_time, p.pet_name, u.fullname AS owner_name, a.service_type, a.status " +
                      "FROM tbl_appointments a " +
                      "JOIN tbl_pet p ON a.pet_id = p.pet_id " +
                      "JOIN tbl_users u ON p.user_id = u.user_id " +
                      "WHERE a.appointment_date = CURDATE() " +
                      "ORDER BY a.appointment_time ASC";
                      
    try (Connection conn = DBConnection.getConnection();
         PreparedStatement pstmt = conn.prepareStatement(tableSql);
         ResultSet rs = pstmt.executeQuery()) {
         
        while (rs.next()) {
            String time = rs.getString("appointment_time");
            String petName = rs.getString("pet_name");
            String owner = rs.getString("owner_name");
            String service = rs.getString("service_type");
            String status = rs.getString("status");
            
            model.addRow(new Object[]{time, petName, owner, service, status});
        }
        
    } catch (Exception e) {
        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Error loading today's table: " + e.getMessage());
    }
}

    private void btnLogoutActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                          
        int choice = javax.swing.JOptionPane.showConfirmDialog(
            this,
            "Are you sure you want to logout?",
            "Logout Confirmation",
            javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_NO_OPTION,
            javax.swing.JOptionPane.QUESTION_MESSAGE
        );

        if (choice == javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_OPTION) {

            UserSession.clearSession();

            adminlogin login = new adminlogin();
            login.setVisible(true);
            this.dispose();
        }
    }

AdminPetsmanage


package batingaanimalmedicalcenterpetappointmentsystem;

import java.sql.Connection;
import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import javax.swing.table.DefaultTableModel;
import javax.swing.JOptionPane;
public class AdminPetmanage extends javax.swing.JFrame {
    
    private static final java.util.logging.Logger logger = java.util.logging.Logger.getLogger(AdminPetmanage.class.getName());

    /**
     * Creates new form AdminPetmanage
     */
    public AdminPetmanage() {
        initComponents();
        loadAllPets();
    }

    private void loadAllPets() {
    DefaultTableModel model = (DefaultTableModel) jTable1.getModel(); // Update to your table variable name if different
    model.setRowCount(0); // Clear existing rows
    
    // SQL query joining tbl_pet and tbl_users to get the owner's full name
    String query = "SELECT p.pet_id, p.pet_name, p.pet_type, p.breed, u.fullname AS owner_name " +
                   "FROM tbl_pet p " +
                   "JOIN tbl_users u ON p.user_id = u.user_id " +
                   "ORDER BY p.pet_id DESC";
                   
    try (Connection conn = DBConnection.getConnection();
         PreparedStatement pstmt = conn.prepareStatement(query);
         ResultSet rs = pstmt.executeQuery()) {
         
        while (rs.next()) {
            int petId = rs.getInt("pet_id");
            String petName = rs.getString("pet_name");
            String species = rs.getString("pet_type");
            String breed = rs.getString("breed");
            String ownerName = rs.getString("owner_name");
            
            // Add row matching your column order: Pet ID, Pet Name, Species, Breed, Owner
            model.addRow(new Object[]{petId, petName, species, breed, ownerName});
        }
        
        // Optional: Hide the Pet ID column (index 0) if you don't want it explicitly shown to the admin visually
        if (jTable1.getColumnCount() > 0) {
            jTable1.getColumnModel().getColumn(0).setMinWidth(0);
            jTable1.getColumnModel().getColumn(0).setMaxWidth(0);
            jTable1.getColumnModel().getColumn(0).setWidth(0);
        }
        
    } catch (Exception e) {
        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Error loading pet records: " + e.getMessage());
        e.printStackTrace();
    }
}

    private void jButton5ActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                         
    adminaddpet addFrame = new adminaddpet();
    addFrame.setVisible(true);
    }                                        

    private void jButton3ActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                         
      int selectedRow = jTable1.getSelectedRow();
if (selectedRow == -1) {
    JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Please select a pet from the table first.", "Selection Required", JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE);
    return;
}

// Get the pet_id from the first column (index 0)
int petId = Integer.parseInt(jTable1.getValueAt(selectedRow, 0).toString());

// Open the view profile frame and pass the pet ID
adminviewprofile viewForm = new adminviewprofile(petId);
viewForm.setVisible(true);
    }                                        

    private void jButton2ActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                         
      int selectedRow = jTable1.getSelectedRow();
if (selectedRow == -1) {
    JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Please select a pet to archive.", "Selection Required", JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE);
    return;
}

int petId = Integer.parseInt(jTable1.getValueAt(selectedRow, 0).toString());

int confirm = JOptionPane.showConfirmDialog(this, "Are you sure you want to archive this pet?", "Confirm Archive", JOptionPane.YES_NO_OPTION);
if (confirm == JOptionPane.YES_OPTION) {
    // You can either delete it: "DELETE FROM tbl_pet WHERE pet_id = ?" 
    // Or update an active status column if you have one. Let's use delete for standard implementation:
    String sql = "DELETE FROM tbl_pet WHERE pet_id = ?";
    
    try (Connection con = DBConnection.getConnection();
         PreparedStatement pst = con.prepareStatement(sql)) {
        
        pst.setInt(1, petId);
        int affected = pst.executeUpdate();
        
        if (affected > 0) {
            JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Pet archived successfully.");
            loadAllPets(); // Refresh table
        }
    } catch (Exception e) {
        JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Error archiving pet: " + e.getMessage());
    }
}
    }                                        

    private void jButton4ActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                         
        int selectedRow = jTable1.getSelectedRow();
if (selectedRow == -1) {
    JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Please select a pet to edit.", "Selection Required", JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE);
    return;
}

int petId = Integer.parseInt(jTable1.getValueAt(selectedRow, 0).toString());
new admineditpet(petId).setVisible(true);

    }

    private void btnLogoutActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                          
        int choice = javax.swing.JOptionPane.showConfirmDialog(
            this,
            "Are you sure you want to logout?",
            "Logout Confirmation",
            javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_NO_OPTION,
            javax.swing.JOptionPane.QUESTION_MESSAGE
        );

        if (choice == javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_OPTION) {

            UserSession.clearSession();

            adminlogin login = new adminlogin();
            login.setVisible(true);
            this.dispose();
        }
    }                                         

    private void jButton6ActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                         
     String searchText = Search.getText().trim(); // Replace jTextFieldSearch with your actual text field variable name
    
    DefaultTableModel model = (DefaultTableModel) jTable1.getModel();
    model.setRowCount(0); // Clear current table rows
    
    // SQL query joining tbl_pet and tbl_users to fetch the owner's full name
    String sql = "SELECT p.pet_id, p.pet_name, p.pet_type, p.breed, u.fullname AS owner_name " +
                 "FROM tbl_pet p " +
                 "JOIN tbl_users u ON p.user_id = u.user_id " +
                 "WHERE p.pet_name LIKE ? OR p.breed LIKE ? OR u.fullname LIKE ?";

    try (java.sql.Connection con = DBConnection.getConnection();
         java.sql.PreparedStatement pst = con.prepareStatement(sql)) {
        
        String searchPattern = "%" + searchText + "%";
        pst.setString(1, searchPattern);
        pst.setString(2, searchPattern);
        pst.setString(3, searchPattern);
        
        try (java.sql.ResultSet rs = pst.executeQuery()) {
            while (rs.next()) {
                int petId = rs.getInt("pet_id");
                String petName = rs.getString("pet_name");
                String petType = rs.getString("pet_type");
                String breed = rs.getString("breed");
                String ownerName = rs.getString("owner_name");
                
                model.addRow(new Object[]{petId, petName, petType, breed, ownerName});
            }
        }
    } catch (Exception e) {
        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Error searching pets: " + e.getMessage(), "Database Error", javax.swing.JOptionPane.ERROR_MESSAGE);
        e.printStackTrace();
    }
    }

package batingaanimalmedicalcenterpetappointmentsystem;


import java.sql.Connection;
import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import javax.swing.table.DefaultTableModel;
import javax.swing.JOptionPane;

public class adminaddpet extends javax.swing.JFrame {
    
    private static final java.util.logging.Logger logger = java.util.logging.Logger.getLogger(adminaddpet.class.getName());

    /**
     * Creates new form adminaddpet
     */
    public adminaddpet() {
        initComponents();
        loadOwners();
    }

    private void loadOwners() {
    cmbOwner.removeAllItems();
    String sql = "SELECT user_id, fullname FROM tbl_users ORDER BY fullname ASC";
    
    try (Connection con = DBConnection.getConnection();
         PreparedStatement pst = con.prepareStatement(sql);
         ResultSet rs = pst.executeQuery()) {
        
        while (rs.next()) {
            int userId = rs.getInt("user_id");
            String fullname = rs.getString("fullname");
            // Store as "ID - Fullname" or use a custom object
            cmbOwner.addItem(userId + " - " + fullname);
        }
    } catch (Exception e) {
        JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Error loading owners: " + e.getMessage());
    }
}

    private void btnSavePetActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                           
 String selectedOwner = (String) cmbOwner.getSelectedItem(); // Assuming your owner dropdown is named cmbOwner
    if (selectedOwner == null || selectedOwner.isEmpty()) {
        JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Please select an owner.", "Validation Error", JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE);
        return;
    }
    
    // Extract user_id from "ID - Full Name" format
    int userId = Integer.parseInt(selectedOwner.split(" - ")[0]);
    
    String petName = txtPetName.getText().trim();
    String petType = (String) cmbPetType.getSelectedItem(); // Dropdown for Dog/Cat/etc.
    String breed = (String) cmbBreed.getSelectedItem();     // Dropdown or text field for breed
    String sex = (String) Petsex.getSelectedItem();         // Dropdown for Male/Female
    String age = txtAge.getText().trim();
    String weight = txtWeight.getText().trim();
    String allergies = txtAllergies.getText().trim();
    
    if (petName.isEmpty()) {
        JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Pet Name is required.", "Validation Error", JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE);
        return;
    }
    
    // SQL insert statement including your extra fields
    String sql = "INSERT INTO tbl_pet (user_id, pet_name, pet_type, breed, sex, age, weight, allergies) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)";
    
    try (Connection con = DBConnection.getConnection();
         PreparedStatement pst = con.prepareStatement(sql)) {
        
        pst.setInt(1, userId);
        pst.setString(2, petName);
        pst.setString(3, petType);
        pst.setString(4, breed);
        pst.setString(5, sex);
        pst.setString(6, age);
        pst.setString(7, weight);
        pst.setString(8, allergies);
        
        int rowsInserted = pst.executeUpdate();
        if (rowsInserted > 0) {
            JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Pet added successfully!");
            new AdminPetmanage().setVisible(true);
            this.dispose(); // Close form after saving
        }
    } catch (Exception e) {
        JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Error adding pet: " + e.getMessage());
        e.printStackTrace();
    }
    }

    private void jButton5ActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                         
 String fullname = JOptionPane.showInputDialog(this, "Enter Client Full Name:");
    if (fullname == null || fullname.trim().isEmpty()) return;

    String contact = JOptionPane.showInputDialog(this, "Enter Contact Number:");
    if (contact == null || contact.trim().isEmpty()) return;

    String address = JOptionPane.showInputDialog(this, "Enter Address:");
    if (address == null || address.trim().isEmpty()) return;

    // Automatically generate a username and a default temporary password
    String username = fullname.toLowerCase().replaceAll("\\s+", "_");
    String tempPassword = "password123"; // Default temporary password for walk-ins

    // Include username and password in the INSERT query
    String sql = "INSERT INTO tbl_users (fullname, contact_number, address, username, password) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)";

    try (Connection con = DBConnection.getConnection();
         PreparedStatement pst = con.prepareStatement(sql, java.sql.Statement.RETURN_GENERATED_KEYS)) {

        pst.setString(1, fullname);
        pst.setString(2, contact);
        pst.setString(3, address);
        pst.setString(4, username);
        pst.setString(5, tempPassword);

        int affected = pst.executeUpdate();
        if (affected > 0) {
            JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "New walk-in client created successfully!\nUsername: " + username + "\nTemp Password: " + tempPassword);
            loadOwners(); // Refresh your dropdown list
        }
    } catch (Exception e) {
        JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Error adding client: " + e.getMessage());
    }



    }

    private void btnLogoutActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                          
        int choice = javax.swing.JOptionPane.showConfirmDialog(
            this,
            "Are you sure you want to logout?",
            "Logout Confirmation",
            javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_NO_OPTION,
            javax.swing.JOptionPane.QUESTION_MESSAGE
        );

        if (choice == javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_OPTION) {

            UserSession.clearSession();

            adminlogin login = new adminlogin();
            login.setVisible(true);
            this.dispose();
        }
    }
