AdminViewprofile


package batingaanimalmedicalcenterpetappointmentsystem;
import java.sql.Connection;
import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import javax.swing.table.DefaultTableModel;
import javax.swing.JOptionPane;
public class adminviewprofile extends javax.swing.JFrame {
    
    private static final java.util.logging.Logger logger = java.util.logging.Logger.getLogger(adminviewprofile.class.getName());

    private int petId;
    
    public adminviewprofile() {
        initComponents();
    }
    
    public adminviewprofile(int petId) {
        initComponents();
        this.petId = petId;
        loadPetProfile();
    }

private void loadPetProfile() {
    String sql = "SELECT p.pet_name, p.pet_type, p.breed, p.weight, p.age, p.allergies, u.fullname AS owner_name " +
                 "FROM tbl_pet p " +
                 "LEFT JOIN tbl_users u ON p.user_id = u.user_id " +
                 "WHERE p.pet_id = ?";

    try (Connection con = DBConnection.getConnection();
         PreparedStatement pst = con.prepareStatement(sql)) {

        pst.setInt(1, petId);
        ResultSet rs = pst.executeQuery();

        if (rs.next()) {
            PetName.setText(rs.getString("pet_name"));
            PetType.setText(rs.getString("pet_type"));
            Breed.setText(rs.getString("breed"));
            Weight.setText(rs.getString("weight"));
            Age.setText(rs.getString("age"));
            Allergies.setText(rs.getString("allergies")); // Set the allergies text
            Owner.setText(rs.getString("owner_name"));
        } else {
            JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Pet profile not found.", "Error", JOptionPane.ERROR_MESSAGE);
        }

    } catch (Exception e) {
        JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Error loading pet profile: " + e.getMessage(), "Database Error", JOptionPane.ERROR_MESSAGE);
    }
}

    private void btnLogoutActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                          
        int choice = javax.swing.JOptionPane.showConfirmDialog(
            this,
            "Are you sure you want to logout?",
            "Logout Confirmation",
            javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_NO_OPTION,
            javax.swing.JOptionPane.QUESTION_MESSAGE
        );

        if (choice == javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_OPTION) {

            UserSession.clearSession();

            adminlogin login = new adminlogin();
            login.setVisible(true);
            this.dispose();
        }
    }

Admineditpets

package batingaanimalmedicalcenterpetappointmentsystem;

import java.sql.Connection;
import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import javax.swing.table.DefaultTableModel;
import javax.swing.JOptionPane;
public class admineditpet extends javax.swing.JFrame {
    
    private static final java.util.logging.Logger logger = java.util.logging.Logger.getLogger(admineditpet.class.getName());

  private int petId;
    public admineditpet() {
        initComponents();
    }

    public admineditpet(int petId) {
        initComponents();
        this.petId = petId;
        loadPetData();
    }
    
private void loadPetData() {
    String sql = "SELECT * FROM tbl_pet WHERE pet_id = ?";
    
    try (Connection con = DBConnection.getConnection();
         PreparedStatement pst = con.prepareStatement(sql)) {
        
        pst.setInt(1, petId);
        ResultSet rs = pst.executeQuery();
        
        if (rs.next()) {
            txtPetName.setText(rs.getString("pet_name"));
            cmbPetType.setSelectedItem(rs.getString("pet_type"));
            cmbBreed.setSelectedItem(rs.getString("breed"));
            Petsex.setSelectedItem(rs.getString("sex")); // Pre-fill sex dropdown
            txtAge.setText(rs.getString("age"));
            txtWeight.setText(rs.getString("weight"));
            txtAllergies.setText(rs.getString("allergies"));
        }
        
    } catch (Exception e) {
        JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Error loading pet details: " + e.getMessage());
    }
}

    private void btnUpdatePetActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                             
     String petName = txtPetName.getText().trim();
        String petType = (cmbPetType.getSelectedItem() != null) ? cmbPetType.getSelectedItem().toString() : "";
        String breed = (cmbBreed.getSelectedItem() != null) ? cmbBreed.getSelectedItem().toString() : "";
        String sex = (Petsex.getSelectedItem() != null) ? Petsex.getSelectedItem().toString() : "";
        String age = txtAge.getText().trim();
        String weight = txtWeight.getText().trim();
        String allergies = txtAllergies.getText().trim();
        
        if (petName.isEmpty()) {
            JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Pet Name cannot be empty.", "Validation Error", JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE);
            return;
        }

        String sql = "UPDATE tbl_pet SET pet_name = ?, pet_type = ?, breed = ?, sex = ?, age = ?, weight = ?, allergies = ? WHERE pet_id = ?";

        try (Connection con = DBConnection.getConnection();
             PreparedStatement pst = con.prepareStatement(sql)) {

            pst.setString(1, petName);
            pst.setString(2, petType);
            pst.setString(3, breed);
            pst.setString(4, sex);
            pst.setString(5, age);
            pst.setString(6, weight);
            pst.setString(7, allergies);
            pst.setInt(8, petId);

            int rowsUpdated = pst.executeUpdate();
            if (rowsUpdated > 0) {
                JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Pet updated successfully!");
                new AdminPetmanage().setVisible(true);
                this.dispose();
            }

        } catch (Exception e) {
            JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Error updating pet: " + e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    private void btnLogoutActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                          
        int choice = javax.swing.JOptionPane.showConfirmDialog(
            this,
            "Are you sure you want to logout?",
            "Logout Confirmation",
            javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_NO_OPTION,
            javax.swing.JOptionPane.QUESTION_MESSAGE
        );

        if (choice == javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_OPTION) {

            UserSession.clearSession();

            adminlogin login = new adminlogin();
            login.setVisible(true);
            this.dispose();
        }
    }

adminappointmentmanagement


package batingaanimalmedicalcenterpetappointmentsystem;

import java.sql.Connection;
import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import javax.swing.table.DefaultTableModel;
import javax.swing.JOptionPane;

public class adminappointmentmanagement extends javax.swing.JFrame {
    
    private static final java.util.logging.Logger logger = java.util.logging.Logger.getLogger(adminappointmentmanagement.class.getName());

    /**
     * Creates new form adminappointmentmanagement
     */
    public adminappointmentmanagement() {
        initComponents();
    }


    private void updateAppointmentStatus(String newStatus) {
    int selectedRow = jTable1.getSelectedRow();
    if (selectedRow == -1) {
        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Please select an appointment from the table first.", "Selection Required", javax.swing.JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE);
        return;
    }

    // Assuming column 5 contains the current status string (Adjust the index if your status column is different)
    String currentStatus = jTable1.getValueAt(selectedRow, 5).toString();

    // RULE: If already Cancelled, it cannot be changed anymore
    if (currentStatus.equalsIgnoreCase("Cancelled")) {
        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "This appointment has already been cancelled and cannot be modified.", "Action Denied", javax.swing.JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE);
        return;
    }

    // RULE: If already Approved, it cannot be rescheduled or modified further (unless you want to allow canceling an approved one, but per your rule: "if it's approved, it can't be rescheduled or cancelled anymore")
    if (currentStatus.equalsIgnoreCase("Approved") && (newStatus.equalsIgnoreCase("Rescheduled") || newStatus.equalsIgnoreCase("Cancelled"))) {
        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Approved appointments cannot be rescheduled or cancelled.", "Action Denied", javax.swing.JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE);
        return;
    }

    int appointmentId = Integer.parseInt(jTable1.getValueAt(selectedRow, 0).toString());

    String sql;
    boolean isReschedule = newStatus.equals("Rescheduled");
    String newDate = "";
    String newTime = "";

    if (isReschedule) {
        newDate = javax.swing.JOptionPane.showInputDialog(this, "Enter new date (YYYY-MM-DD):", "Reschedule Appointment", javax.swing.JOptionPane.QUESTION_MESSAGE);
        if (newDate == null || newDate.trim().isEmpty()) return;

        newTime = javax.swing.JOptionPane.showInputDialog(this, "Enter new time (e.g., 2:00 PM):", "Reschedule Appointment", javax.swing.JOptionPane.QUESTION_MESSAGE);
        if (newTime == null || newTime.trim().isEmpty()) return;

        sql = "UPDATE tbl_appointments SET appointment_date = ?, appointment_time = ?, status = ? WHERE appointment_id = ?";
    } else {
        sql = "UPDATE tbl_appointments SET status = ? WHERE appointment_id = ?";
    }

    try (java.sql.Connection con = DBConnection.getConnection();
         java.sql.PreparedStatement pst = con.prepareStatement(sql)) {
        
        if (isReschedule) {
            pst.setString(1, newDate.trim());
            pst.setString(2, newTime.trim());
            pst.setString(3, newStatus);
            pst.setInt(4, appointmentId);
        } else {
            pst.setString(1, newStatus);
            pst.setInt(2, appointmentId);
        }

        int rowsUpdated = pst.executeUpdate();
        if (rowsUpdated > 0) {
            javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Appointment status updated to '" + newStatus + "' successfully!");
            // Call your table refresh method here, e.g., loadAppointments();
        }

    } catch (Exception e) {
        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Error updating status: " + e.getMessage(), "Database Error", javax.swing.JOptionPane.ERROR_MESSAGE);
        e.printStackTrace();
    }
}

    }

    }                                        

    private void jButton5ActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                         
        java.util.Date selectedDate = jDateChooser1.getDate(); 
    
    if (selectedDate == null) {
        JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Please select a date from the calendar first.", "Validation Error", JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE);
        return;
    }

    // Format the date to match MySQL date format (YYYY-MM-DD)
    java.text.SimpleDateFormat sdf = new java.text.SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd");
    String dateStr = sdf.format(selectedDate);

    // 2. Query to fetch appointments matching the selected date
    // Note: Double-check that your table and column names match your database schema
String sql = "SELECT a.appointment_id, p.pet_name, u.fullname AS owner_name, a.service_type, a.appointment_time, a.status, a.reason_for_visit " +
             "FROM tbl_appointments a " +
             "JOIN tbl_pet p ON a.pet_id = p.pet_id " +
             "JOIN tbl_users u ON p.user_id = u.user_id " +
             "WHERE a.appointment_date = ?";

    try (Connection con = DBConnection.getConnection();
         PreparedStatement pst = con.prepareStatement(sql)) {

        pst.setString(1, dateStr);
        ResultSet rs = pst.executeQuery();

        // 3. Populate your JTable model
        javax.swing.table.DefaultTableModel model = (javax.swing.table.DefaultTableModel) jTable1.getModel();
        model.setRowCount(0); // Clear existing table rows

        boolean found = false;
        while (rs.next()) {
            found = true;
            model.addRow(new Object[]{
    rs.getInt("appointment_id"),
    rs.getString("pet_name"),
    rs.getString("owner_name"),
    rs.getString("service_type"),      // Match your actual DB column
    rs.getString("appointment_time"),  // Match your actual DB column
    rs.getString("status"),
    rs.getString("reason_for_visit")
            });
        }

        if (!found) {
            JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "No appointments found for " + dateStr, "Information", JOptionPane.INFORMATION_MESSAGE);
        }

    } catch (Exception e) {
        JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Error searching appointments: " + e.getMessage(), "Database Error", JOptionPane.ERROR_MESSAGE);
    }
    }                                        

    private void jButton6ActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                         
int selectedRow = jTable1.getSelectedRow();
    if (selectedRow == -1) {
        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Please select an appointment first.", "Selection Required", javax.swing.JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE);
        return;
    }

    // 1. Get the status from column index 5
    String status = jTable1.getValueAt(selectedRow, 5).toString();

    // 2. STRICTLY ALLOW ONLY IF APPROVED (Blocks Completed, Pending, Cancelled)
    if (!status.equalsIgnoreCase("Approved")) {
        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this, 
            "Visit records can only be added for 'Approved' appointments. Current status is: " + status, 
            "Action Denied", 
            javax.swing.JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE
        );
        return; // Stops execution and prevents opening the window
    }

    // 3. Extract details and open visit records if approved
    String appointmentId = jTable1.getValueAt(selectedRow, 0).toString();
    String petName = jTable1.getValueAt(selectedRow, 1).toString();
    String owner = jTable1.getValueAt(selectedRow, 2).toString();
    String service = jTable1.getValueAt(selectedRow, 3).toString();
    String time = jTable1.getValueAt(selectedRow, 4).toString();
    String reason = jTable1.getValueAt(selectedRow, 6).toString();

    String petId = "";
    String query = "SELECT pet_id, appointment_date FROM tbl_appointments WHERE appointment_id = ?";
    String date = "";
    
    try (java.sql.Connection con = DBConnection.getConnection();
         java.sql.PreparedStatement pst = con.prepareStatement(query)) {
        
        pst.setString(1, appointmentId);
        try (java.sql.ResultSet rs = pst.executeQuery()) {
            if (rs.next()) {
                petId = rs.getString("pet_id");
                date = rs.getString("appointment_date");
            }
        }
    } catch (Exception e) {
        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Error fetching appointment details: " + e.getMessage());
        return;
    }

    // Open the Visit Records window
    adminvisitrecords visitPage = new adminvisitrecords(appointmentId, petId, petName, owner, date, reason);
    visitPage.setVisible(true);
    this.dispose();
    }                                        

    private void btnDashboardActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                             
      Adminhome home = new Adminhome();
        home.setVisible(true);
        this.dispose();
    }                                            

    private void btnMyPetsActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                          
        AdminPetmanage manage = new AdminPetmanage();
        manage.setVisible(true);
        this.dispose();
    }                                         

    private void btnBookAppointmentActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                                   

        adminappointmentmanagement app = new adminappointmentmanagement();
        app.setVisible(true);
        this.dispose();
    }                                                  

    private void btnLogout1ActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                           
        adminreports reports = new adminreports();
        reports.setVisible(true);
        this.dispose();

    }                                          

    private void btnLogoutActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                          
        int choice = javax.swing.JOptionPane.showConfirmDialog(
            this,
            "Are you sure you want to logout?",
            "Logout Confirmation",
            javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_NO_OPTION,
            javax.swing.JOptionPane.QUESTION_MESSAGE
        );

        if (choice == javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_OPTION) {

            UserSession.clearSession();

            adminlogin login = new adminlogin();
            login.setVisible(true);
            this.dispose();
        }
    }

    this.storedPetId = Integer.parseInt(petId);
    
    // Set the text to your specific GUI component variable names
    PetName1.setText(petName);
    Owner1.setText(owner);
    Date1.setText(date);
    ReasonforVisit.setText(reason);
    }

    private void jButton6ActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                         
        // 1. Get text values from your fields
String veterinarian = txtVeterinarian.getText().trim();
String diagnosis = txtDiagnosis.getText().trim();
String treatment = txtTreatment.getText().trim();

if (veterinarian.isEmpty() || diagnosis.isEmpty() || treatment.isEmpty()) {
    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Please fill in all required visit record fields.", "Validation Error", javax.swing.JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE);
    return;
}

// 2. Database queries to insert the visit record and mark the appointment as 'Completed'
String insertVisitSql = "INSERT INTO tbl_visit_records (pet_id, appointment_id, visit_date, diagnosis, treatment, veterinarian) VALUES (?, ?, CURDATE(), ?, ?, ?)";
String updateAppointmentSql = "UPDATE tbl_appointments SET status = 'Completed' WHERE appointment_id = ?";

try (java.sql.Connection con = DBConnection.getConnection()) {
    
    // Disable auto-commit to run both operations as a transaction
    con.setAutoCommit(false);

    try (java.sql.PreparedStatement pstVisit = con.prepareStatement(insertVisitSql);
         java.sql.PreparedStatement pstAppt = con.prepareStatement(updateAppointmentSql)) {
        
        // Assuming you stored `petId` and `appointmentId` as variables in this class when passing data from the appointment list:
        pstVisit.setInt(1, storedPetId);       // Replace with your actual pet ID variable
        pstVisit.setInt(2, storedAppointmentId); // Replace with your actual appointment ID variable
        pstVisit.setString(3, diagnosis);
        pstVisit.setString(4, treatment);
        pstVisit.setString(5, veterinarian);
        pstVisit.executeUpdate();

        pstAppt.setInt(1, storedAppointmentId);
        pstAppt.executeUpdate();

        con.commit(); // Commit both queries successfully

        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Visit record added successfully and appointment marked as Completed!");
        
        adminappointmentmanagement apptManagement = new adminappointmentmanagement();
        apptManagement.setVisible(true);
        this.dispose();
        
        // Optionally, close this window or return to appointment management
        this.dispose();

    } catch (Exception ex) {
        con.rollback(); // Roll back if anything fails
        throw ex;
    }

} catch (Exception e) {
    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Error saving visit record: " + e.getMessage(), "Database Error", javax.swing.JOptionPane.ERROR_MESSAGE);
    e.printStackTrace();
}
    }

    private void btnLogoutActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                          
        int choice = javax.swing.JOptionPane.showConfirmDialog(
            this,
            "Are you sure you want to logout?",
            "Logout Confirmation",
            javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_NO_OPTION,
            javax.swing.JOptionPane.QUESTION_MESSAGE
        );

        if (choice == javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_OPTION) {

            UserSession.clearSession();

            adminlogin login = new adminlogin();
            login.setVisible(true);
            this.dispose();
        }
    }

    private int getStatusCount(java.sql.Connection con, String sql, String dateFrom, String dateTo, String status) throws java.sql.SQLException {
    try (java.sql.PreparedStatement pst = con.prepareStatement(sql)) {
        pst.setString(1, dateFrom);
        pst.setString(2, dateTo);
        pst.setString(3, status);
        try (java.sql.ResultSet rs = pst.executeQuery()) {
            if (rs.next()) {
                return rs.getInt(1);
            }
        }
    }
    return 0;
}

    private void btnCancelActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                          
    if (jDateChooserFrom.getDate() == null || jDateChooserTo.getDate() == null) {
        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Please select both 'Date From' and 'Date To' ranges.", "Date Required", javax.swing.JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE);
        return;
    }

    // 2. Format the dates to match your MySQL database format (YYYY-MM-DD)
    java.text.SimpleDateFormat sdf = new java.text.SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd");
    String dateFrom = sdf.format(jDateChooserFrom.getDate());
    String dateTo = sdf.format(jDateChooserTo.getDate());

    // 3. SQL Queries to count total and status breakdowns within the date range
    String totalSql = "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tbl_appointments WHERE appointment_date BETWEEN ? AND ?";
    String statusSql = "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tbl_appointments WHERE appointment_date BETWEEN ? AND ? AND status = ?";

    try (java.sql.Connection con = DBConnection.getConnection()) {
        
        // Get Total Appointments
        int total = 0;
        try (java.sql.PreparedStatement pst = con.prepareStatement(totalSql)) {
            pst.setString(1, dateFrom);
            pst.setString(2, dateTo);
            try (java.sql.ResultSet rs = pst.executeQuery()) {
                if (rs.next()) {
                    total = rs.getInt(1);
                }
            }
        }

        // Get Status Counts
        int completed = getStatusCount(con, statusSql, dateFrom, dateTo, "Completed");
        int cancelled = getStatusCount(con, statusSql, dateFrom, dateTo, "Cancelled");
        int pending = getStatusCount(con, statusSql, dateFrom, dateTo, "Pending");

        // 4. Update your text labels (replace lblTotal, lblCompleted, etc., with your actual label names)
        Total.setText(String.valueOf(total));
        Completed.setText(String.valueOf(completed));
        Cancelled.setText(String.valueOf(cancelled));
        Pending.setText(String.valueOf(pending));

    } catch (Exception e) {
        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(this, "Error generating report: " + e.getMessage(), "Database Error", javax.swing.JOptionPane.ERROR_MESSAGE);
        e.printStackTrace();
    }
    }

    private void btnLogoutActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {                                          
        int choice = javax.swing.JOptionPane.showConfirmDialog(
            this,
            "Are you sure you want to logout?",
            "Logout Confirmation",
            javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_NO_OPTION,
            javax.swing.JOptionPane.QUESTION_MESSAGE
        );

        if (choice == javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_OPTION) {

            UserSession.clearSession();

            adminlogin login = new adminlogin();
            login.setVisible(true);
            this.dispose();
        }
    }
